local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- Parse watchlist
local watchlist = {}   -- [userId] = country
for line in DATA:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
    line = line:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if line ~= "" then
        local uid, country = line:match("^(%d+)%s*:%s*(.+)$")
        if uid then
            country = country:gsub("%s*%d+x%s*$", "")
            country = country:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            watchlist[tonumber(uid)] = country
        end
    end
end
local function countList() local n=0 for _ in pairs(watchlist) do n=n+1 end return n end
print("[CountryWatcher] Loaded " .. countList() .. " tracked players.")

-- ===== COMPAT HELPERS =====
local function safeCall(fn, ...) 
    local ok, a, b = pcall(fn, ...)
    if ok then return a, b end
    return nil
end

local function getRootChar(player)
    local char = player.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end

-- ===== GET COUNTRY TAG (from game's overhead UI) =====
local function getCountryTag(player)
    local ok1, tag = pcall(function()
        return player:FindFirstChild("Country") or player:FindFirstChild("CountryTag")
    end)
    if ok1 and tag and tag:IsA("StringValue") and tag.Value ~= "" then
        return tag.Value
    end

    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, bb in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                    if bb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        for _, label in pairs(bb:GetDescendants()) do
                            if label:IsA("TextLabel") and label.Text then
                                local t = label.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                                if #t >= 2 and #t <= 3 and t:upper() == t and t:match("^%u%u") then
                                    return t
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- ===== OVERHEAD LABELS =====
local overheads = {} -- [player] = holder

local function createOverhead(player)
    if player == LocalPlayer then return end
    local trackedCountry = watchlist[player.UserId]
    if not trackedCountry then return end
    if overheads[player] then return end

    local holder = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    holder.Name = "CountryWatcherTag"
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
    holder.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
    holder.AlwaysOnTop = true
    holder.MaxDistance = 1000

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Label"
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = trackedCountry .. " (?)"
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = holder

    overheads[player] = holder

    local function attach(char)
        task.spawn(function()
            local t0 = tick()
            local head
            while tick() - t0 < 10 and not head do
                head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if not head then task.wait(0.1) end
            end
            if head and overheads[player] == holder then
                pcall(function() holder.Parent = head end)
            end
        end)
    end

    if player.Character then attach(player.Character) end
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        pcall(function() holder.Parent = nil end)
        attach(char)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while overheads[player] == holder and player.Parent do
            local ok, country = pcall(getCountryTag, player)
            country = ok and country or nil
            if country then
                label.Text = trackedCountry .. " | " .. country
                label.TextColor3 = (country:upper() == trackedCountry:upper())
                    and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
            else
                label.Text = trackedCountry .. " (?)"
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
        pcall(function() holder:Destroy() end)
        overheads[player] = nil
    end)
end

for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do task.spawn(createOverhead, p) end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p) task.wait(1) createOverhead(p) refreshList() end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
    if overheads[p] then pcall(function() overheads[p]:Destroy() end) overheads[p] = nil end
    if watchlist[p.UserId] then
        print("[CountryWatcher] TRACKED player left: " .. p.Name .. " (" .. watchlist[p.UserId] .. ")")
    end
    refreshList()
end)

-- ===== FEATURE STATE =====
local viewing = nil        -- player currently viewed
local viewConn = nil
local spamTPPlayer = nil
local spamTPOn = false

local function stopView()
    if viewConn then pcall(function() viewConn:Disconnect() end) viewConn = nil end
    viewing = nil
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() cam.CameraSubject = hum end) end
        pcall(function() cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
    end
end

local function startView(player)
    stopView()
    viewing = player
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    pcall(function() cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable end)
    viewConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not viewing or not viewing.Parent then stopView() return end
        local root = getRootChar(viewing)
        local cam2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if root and cam2 then
            pcall(function()
                cam2.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 8, 12), root.Position)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

local function tpTo(player)
    local myRoot = getRootChar(LocalPlayer)
    local theirRoot = getRootChar(player)
    if myRoot and theirRoot then
        pcall(function()
            myRoot.CFrame = theirRoot.CFrame + theirRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 3 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
        end)
    end
end

-- ===== GUI =====
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "CountryWatcherGui"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
if not gui.Parent then gui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

local main = Instance.new("Frame")
main.Name = "Main"
main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 340)
main.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -170)
main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
main.BorderSizePixel = 0
main.Active = true
main.Parent = gui
Instance.new("UICorner", main).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Text = "👀 Country Watcher"
title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120)
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = 15
title.Parent = main

-- Draggable (works on both executors via InputBegan/InputChanged)
do
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    title.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = main.Position
        end
    end)
    local uis = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    uis.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end)
    uis.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
end

-- Scrollable player list
local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -130)
scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 34)
scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 42)
scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
scroll.Parent = main
Instance.new("UICorner", scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", scroll)
listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", scroll)
pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6); pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6); pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)

local selectedPlayer = nil
local selectedLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
selectedLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
selectedLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -88)
selectedLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
selectedLabel.Text = "Selected: none"
selectedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
selectedLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
selectedLabel.TextSize = 13
selectedLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
selectedLabel.Parent = main

-- Buttons
local function makeButton(text, yPos, color)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 24)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, yPos)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = color
    b.Text = text
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 13
    b.Parent = main
    Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    return b
end

local tpBtn      = makeButton("⚡ TP", -60, Color3.fromRGB(40, 110, 60))
local spamTpBtn  = makeButton("🔁 Spam TP: OFF", -32, Color3.fromRGB(110, 80, 30))
local viewBtn    = makeButton("🎥 View", -4, Color3.fromRGB(50, 70, 130))

local function getSelected()
    if selectedPlayer and selectedPlayer.Parent then return selectedPlayer end
    return nil
end

tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local p = getSelected()
    if p then tpTo(p) end
end)

spamTpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local p = getSelected()
    if not p then return end
    if spamTPOn and spamTPPlayer == p then
        spamTPOn = false
        spamTpBtn.Text = "🔁 Spam TP: OFF"
        spamTpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 80, 30)
    else
        spamTPPlayer = p
        spamTPOn = true
        spamTpBtn.Text = "🔁 Spam TP: ON (" .. p.Name .. ")"
        spamTpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 40)
    end
end)

viewBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local p = getSelected()
    if not p then return end
    if viewing == p then
        stopView()
        viewBtn.Text = "🎥 View"
    else
        startView(p)
        viewBtn.Text = "🎥 Stop View"
    end
end)

-- spam TP loop
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if spamTPOn and spamTPPlayer and spamTPPlayer.Parent then
            tpTo(spamTPPlayer)
        elseif spamTPOn and (not spamTPPlayer or not spamTPPlayer.Parent) then
            spamTPOn = false
            spamTpBtn.Text = "🔁 Spam TP: OFF"
            spamTpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 80, 30)
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

-- ===== PLAYER LIST (live update, only watchlist players) =====
local rows = {}

local function refreshList()
    -- clear old rows
    for _, r in pairs(rows) do pcall(function() r:Destroy() end) end
    rows = {}
    if selectedPlayer and (not selectedPlayer.Parent or not watchlist[selectedPlayer.UserId]) then
        selectedPlayer = nil
        selectedLabel.Text = "Selected: none"
    end
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local country = watchlist[player.UserId]
        if country and player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)
            row.Text = ""
            row.AutoButtonColor = true
            row.Parent = scroll
            Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

            local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0)
            nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
            nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            -- display name + username + country
            local dn = player.DisplayName
            local un = player.Name
            nameLbl.Text = (dn == un and un or dn .. " (@" .. un .. ")") .. "  •  " .. country
            nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
            nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            nameLbl.TextSize = 12
            nameLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            nameLbl.Parent = row

            row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selectedPlayer = player
                selectedLabel.Text = "Selected: " .. dn .. " (@" .. un .. ")"
                for otherR, otherLbl in pairs(rows) do
                    -- highlight handling below
                end
                -- simple highlight: recolor all rows
                for _, r2 in pairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
                    if r2:IsA("TextButton") then
                        r2.BackgroundColor3 = (r2 == row) and Color3.fromRGB(60, 110, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 55)
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(rows, row)
        end
    end
end

-- live refresh every 2s (catches joins/leaves and name changes)
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        refreshList()
        task.wait(2)
    end
end)
refreshList()

print("[CountryWatcher] GUI loaded — tracked players only. Drag by the title bar.")
